# Graph Report - wt-121  (2026-10-08)

## Corpus Check
- 1648 files · ~3,913,617 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 95 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .mmd 38, (none) 34, .jsonl 5)

## Summary
- 39106 nodes · 93606 edges · 1299 communities (1080 shown, 219 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 91% EXTRACTED · 9% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8630 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `27ab83ad`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
